# Aggregated political leader scripted effects [A-F], merged from per-country files.

# --- AFG_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_AFG = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 10 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Kingdom_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Kingdom_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = AFG_gulbuddin_hekmatyar
				picture = "Gulbuddin_Hekmatyar.dds"
				desc = "GULBUDDIN_HEKMATYAR_DESC"
				ideology = Kingdom
				traits = {
					guerrilla_leader
					salafist_Kingdom
					stubborn
					narcissist
					afghanistan_butcher_of_kabul
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Kingdom_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 12 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = AFG_burhanuddin_rabbani
				picture = "Burhanuddin_Rabbani.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood
				traits = {
					cleric
					neutrality_Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood
					stout_defender
					afghanistan_support_afghan_peace
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = AFG_ahmad_zia_massoud
				picture = "Ahmad_Zia_Massoud.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood
				traits = {
					career_politician
					neutrality_Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 2 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = AFG_mohammad_qasim_fahim
				picture = "Mohammad_Qasim_Fahim.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood
				traits = {
					military_career
					neutrality_Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 3 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
		add_to_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 1 }
		hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

		create_country_leader = {
			name = AFG_yunus_qanuni
			picture = "yunus_qanuni.dds"
			ideology = Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood
			traits = {
				career_politician
				neutrality_Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood
			}
		}

		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 4 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = AFG_atta_muhammad_nur
				picture = "Atta_Muhammad_Nur.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood
				traits = {
					military_career
					neutrality_Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood
					greedy
					ruthless
					afghanistan_king_of_the_north
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 5 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = AFG_mohammad_ismail_khan
				picture = "Mohammad_Ismail_Khan.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood
				traits = {
					military_career
					neutrality_Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood
					afghanistan_lion_of_herat
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 6 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = AFG_salahuddin_rabbani
				picture = "salahuddin_rabbani.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood
				traits = {

			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_leader = 7 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }

			create_country_leader = {

# --- AFR_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_AFR = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 22 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_afr_johnny_paul_koroma
				picture = "johnny_koroma.dds"
				ideology = Nat_Autocracy
				traits = {
					nationalist_Nat_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 13 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_afr_foday_sankoh
				picture = "Foday_Sankoh.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_Autocracy
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
}

# --- AGL_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_AGL = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_agl_isaias_samakuva
				picture = "isaias_samakuvaa.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_agl_analia_de_victoria_pereira
				picture = "Analia_de_Victoria_Pereira.dds"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 3 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { socialism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_agl_bengui_pedro_joao
				picture = "Bengui_Pedro_Joao.dds"
				ideology = socialism

					western_socialism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 5 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { anarchist_communism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { anarchist_communism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_agl_luis_da_silva_dos_passos
				picture = "Luis_da_Silva_dos_Passos.dds"
				ideology = anarchist_communism
				traits = {
					emerging_anarchist_communism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { anarchist_communism_leader = 1 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 7 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Autocracy_leader = 1 }

				}
			}

			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 2 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 2 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			set_temp_variable = { b = 2 }
		}
	}

set_leader_ARM = {
	if = { limit = { western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ARM_paruyr_hayrikyan
				picture = "paruyr_hayrikyan.dds"
				ideology = Western_Autocracy
				traits = {
					western_Western_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ARM_zapadnye_sos } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 } OR = { has_country_flag = ARM_zapadnye_sos has_country_flag = ARM_zapadnye_polyarniki } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ARM_jirayr_sefilyan
				picture = "jirayr_sefilyan.dds"
				ideology = Western_Autocracy
				traits = {
					western_Western_Autocracy
				}
			}

		# 文莱绝对君主制：与 SAU 的 Kingdom 槽同口径（ideology = Kingdom，trait = salafist_Kingdom）

				traits = {
					king

				}
			}

			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# --- CAS_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_CAS = {

	##########################
	##### Western Autocrats #####
	##########################
	if = { limit = { western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = CAS_bill_bryant
				picture = "Bill_Bryant.dds"
				ideology = Western_Autocracy
				traits = {
					western_Western_Autocracy
					capable
					diplomat
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
		}
	}

	##########################
	##### Western Conservatism #####
	##########################
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = CAS_dave_reichert
				picture = "Dave_Reichert.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
					military_career
					rational
					popular_figurehead
				}

			add_to_variable = { Neutral_Libertarian_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = CBD_sam_rainsy
				picture = "sam_rainsy.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_Libertarian
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_Libertarian
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_Libertarian_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 23 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = CBD_norodom_sihanouk
				picture = "norodom_sihanouk.dds"
				expire = "2004.1.1" #Historically Abdiacted in 2004
				ideology = Monarchist
				traits = {
					nationalist_Monarchist
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2004.1.1 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2004
		}
	}
}

CHE_ahmat_ai_popularity_bump = {
	if = {
		limit = { CHE = { is_ai = yes } }
		set_temp_variable = { party_index = 6 }
		set_temp_variable = { party_popularity_increase = 0.15 }
		set_temp_variable = { temp_outlook_increase = 0.15 }
		change_relative_party_popularity = yes
	}
}

			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { communist_state_leader = 7 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { communist_state_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = CHI_wang_zheng
				picture = "Wang_Zheng_neo-maoism.dds"
				ideology = communist_state
				traits = {
					emerging_communist_state
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { communist_state_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017

                }
            }
        }
    }
}

set_leader_FSA = {

	if = { limit = { western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_fsa_farid_ghadry
				picture = "farid_ghadry.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_Western_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_fsa_haytham_manna
				picture = "Westerncomp_Haytham_Manna.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism 
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		} 
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

				name = ZZZ_nm_fsa_anas_al_abdah

					western_liberalism
				}

	}

				picture = "riyad_al_turk.dds"

				traits = {
					western_socialism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_fsa_george_sabra
				picture = "george_sabra.dds"
				ideology = socialism
				traits = {
					western_socialism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		} 
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 4 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Communist-State_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Communist-State_leader = 1 }

				picture = "hanin_nimir.dds"

		}  
	}
